Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on stationarity structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 479. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 792 with relations in degree 3.

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_479(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 479. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^792(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 216.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Let the stationarity structure S satisfy the conditions of structural decomposition framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 636. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 521 and converges at rate O(1/n^4).

Proof: We apply the structural decomposition principle to the stationarity decomposition. By minimality, the element a^636 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 521-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
